All Gold Skulltula locations in Ocarina of Time

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

How to save the cursed family in Kakariko Village

Gold Skulltulas act as a collectible in The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time remake, but they're also needed to cure a cursed family in Kakariko Village.

The poor family have been turned into Skulltulas, and the only way to cleanse them of their curse is to hunt down the Gold Skulltulas scattered across Hyrule. However, that's easier said than done, as there are 100 of them in total, and they're extremely difficult to find.

Here's where to find every Gold Skulltula in Ocarina of Time remake, and the rewards for curing the family in Kakariko Village.

[Ed. note: This guide contains spoilers for The Legend of Zelda: Ocarina of Time remake, so continue at your own risk! Feel free to follow along or bookmark this page and come back later.]

As you collect Gold Skulltula tokens, you can turn them in to the cursed family in Kakariko Village for rewards. Every 10 Gold Skulltulas you collect from 10 to 50 will lead to a reward, but then you won't receive anything until you turn in all 100 Gold Skulltulas. After you collect 20 Gold Skulltulas, make sure to get your reward, as the Echo Gem is extremely helpful for finding the rest of them.

Below, we've listed all the Gold Skulltula rewards, and how many you need to turn in to receive them.

This big wallet, made for grown-ups, is able to hold up to 500 rupees.

Equipping this gem will help you detect nearby Gold Skulltulas through a combination of light and sound.

This enormous wallet is able to hold up to 1,000 rupees.

Once placed on the ground, this wall-climbing bomb zooms off on its own before exploding after a certain distance.

Collect four Pieces of Heart to gain a heart.

Every time you talk to the Restored Rich Man, he'll give you a gold rupee.

There are 100 Gold Skulltula tokens in Ocarina of Time. We've listed where to find every Gold Skulltula separated by zone. In each section, the Gold Skulltulas are listed in chronological order.

If you're looking for a specific area, make sure to use the Ctrl + F shortcut and search for its name.

[Ed. note: If you're viewing this guide on a mobile device, you may have to swipe left and right to see all the information on the tables below.]

In this section, we'll cover the 16 Gold Skulltulas found in the following areas:

In the room with the compass, look along the southern wall to find this Gold Skulltula.

Right after you fall into the giant spider web in the main room and land in the water, turn around to find this Gold Skulltula crawling on the vines.

Immediately after you grab the Gold Skulltula above, head into the next room, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula crawling on metal bars by the water. Shoot it with your Fairy Slingshot, and jump at the token to collect it.

In the room in the top left corner of B1, you'll find a Gold Skulltula climbing on vines. Be careful of the Deku Baba and Skulltula along the way!

Head to the east side of Kokiri Shop and place a bug onto the pile of soft soil to force a Gold Skulltula to pop out.

Go to the west side of the Know-It-All Brother's House at night, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula.

From the Kokiri Forest entrance, take the path to the left, and then take another left. You'll find a pile of soft soil on your left. Place a bug on it, and a Gold Skulltula will pop out.

From the Kokiri Forest entrance, go right, left, right, left, and left. Put a bug onto the soft soil and a Gold Skulltula will come out.

Go behind the House of Twins, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula. Use your Hookshot to retrieve its token.

From the Kokiri Forest entrance, go right, left, right, left, and left to return to the Magic Bean you planted. Get onto it, and it'll take you to a Gold Skulltula.

Head into the Sacred Grove, and progress until you reach the square-shaped path. Follow the path to the east, and look at the wall to spot a Gold Skulltula. Use your Hookshot to retrieve its token.

In the first room of the Forest Temple, look up at the tree on your right and you'll see a Gold Skulltula on the vines. Climb up to the branch, and use your Hookshot to retrieve the token.

After you enter the main room on the first floor, head up the stairs to the north. Turn around to find this Gold Skulltula on the wall to the southeast.

After you drop into the hole at the end of the untwisted red hallway, go through the door, which will take you to a balcony. Follow the path to the left of the southern door, and you'll see a Gold Skulltula on the wall to the south.

After you defeat the Poe sisters, ride the elevator in the main room. Enter the southern room to see a Gold Skulltula on the wall, but there will be a Skulltula that will block your path.

Return to the Forest Temple after you learn the Scarecrow Song. If you pushed the stone cube at the end of the checkered room, you can head straight into the northeast room and climb onto the stone cube. If not, you need to make your way to the checkered room and follow the hallway to the right. Play the Scarecrow Song, and a scarecrow will appear on the balcony above you. Hookshot or Longshot to it, and there will be a Gold Skulltula on the ground.

In this section, we'll cover the six Gold Skulltulas found in the following locations:

Roll into the tree just west of the river near the Kokiri Forest entrance, and a Gold Skulltula will fall out.

Roll into the tree on the east side of Talon and Malon's house at Lon Lon Ranch, and a Gold Skulltula will fall out.

Bombs, Boomerang (young Link), Hookshot (adult Link)

Break the circular rock in the ground in the southern trees in Hyrule Field. Defeat the Skulltula inside, and then hit the Gold Skulltula on the back wall with a Boomerang or Hookshot.

On the western side of Talon and Malon's house, you'll see a Gold Skulltula on the window. Use your Boomerang to collect it.

Night, Boomerang (Young Link), Hookshot (Adult Link)

Go into the stone tower in the southwest corner of Lon Lon Ranch. Look up, and you'll see a Gold Skulltula resting on some vines. Use your Boomerang or Hookshot to retrieve its token.

Break the circular rock as young Link, and drop into the hole. Defeat the Skullwalltula on the back wall and travel into the future. Return to the cave, and you'll find a box. Roll into it and a Gold Skulltula will pop out.

In this section, we'll cover the three Gold Skulltulas found in the following areas:

After you enter Hyrule Castle Town, immediately turn right and go into the door ahead to enter the Guard Tower. At the end of the room, a box will periodically jump up and down. Roll into the box to break it, which will reveal a Gold Skulltula.

Before you climb up the vines in the garden outside of Hyrule Castle, turn to the right to see a lone tree. Roll into it, and a Gold Skulltula will drop onto the ground.

Young Link, Bombs, Boomerang, Song of Storms

Play the Song of Storms by the tree by Hyrule Castle's moat. Drop into the hole, and blow up the rock wall to your right. There will be a Skullwalltula and a Gold Skulltula on the wall. Hit it with your Boomerang to retrieve it.

In this section, we'll cover the 16 Gold Skulltulas in the following areas:

Go behind the northwest house in Kakariko Village at nighttime, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula on the wall.

This Gold Skulltula is found on the ladder of the tall tower in Kakariko Village at night.

Roll into the tree at the center of Kakariko Village, and a Gold Skulltula will drop onto the ground.

Near the center of Kakariko Village, you'll find a house that is under construction. Come here at night to find a Gold Skulltula on the wall.

This Gold Skulltula can be found just beside the door of the Skulltula House.

Head to the graveyard with a bug in a bottle. In the northwest corner, you'll find some soft soil. Let the bug out of the bottle onto the soft soil, and a Gold Skulltula will pop out.

Night, Boomerang (young Link), Hookshot (adult Link)

This Gold Skulltula is found behind Dampé's house. Use your Boomerang or Hookshot to retrieve it.

Hookshot onto the vines to the southeast of Impa's house (the southernmost house in Kakariko Village), and jump onto the roof. You'll find a Gold Skulltula on the wall to the south. Hookshot it to retrieve it.

Use the Lenses of Truth to avoid any holes in the floor as you navigate your way into the eastern room. You'll enter a room with a Like Like, but there is also a Gold Skulltula on the wall. Use the Boomerang to retrieve it.

Use the Lenses of Truth to jump into the central room. Use a small key to open the east door, and use the Lenses of Truth to spot an invisible pathway. Walk across it, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula in the corner of the room.

Use the Lenses of Truth to jump into the central room. Use a small key to open the west door, and you'll find a Deku Baba and a Gold Skulltula on the wall.

After you cross the bridge of guillotines on B3 and defeat the Stalfos at the end, put on the Lenses of Truth and look to the east. Put on the Hover Boots, cross the gap, and go into the room. Use the Lenses of Truth to see a scythe trap. Avoid it, and go into the next room to find a Gold Skulltula.

Enter the southwestern room on B3, and put on the Lenses of Truth to see a block hidden inside the wall. Use it to stop the spike traps from crushing you, and then climb on top of them. Ride them up, and you'll see a Gold Skulltula in the northeast corner of the room.

After you Longshot in the hallway with the fans and Blade Traps, turn around to find a Gold Skulltula on the wall below you. Use your Longshot to retrieve its token.

After you ride the elevator down to B4, pull the stone cube out of the wall and climb onto the wooden platform. Look to the caged area, and play the Scarecrow Song. Longshot to the scarecrow, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula.

Adult Link, Bombs, Fire Arrow or Din's Fire, Lenses of Truth

After you get off the boat on B4, set fire to the spider web covering the door. Use the Lenses of Truth to make it through the invisible maze, and enter the west door. Throw a bomb into all three rotating skulls, and a Gold Skulltula will pop out of the last one.

In this section, we'll cover the 18 Gold Skulltulas in the following areas:

Blow up the entrance to Dodongo Cavern using the bomb flower above. At the entrance, you'll find a mound of soft soil. Release a bug onto it, and a Gold Skulltula will jump out.

After you first make it to the second floor in Dodongo Cavern, you'll find this Gold Skulltula on a wall at the top of the stairs.

After you unlock the Bomb Bag and return to the second floor of the main room in Dodongo Cavern, turn to the right to find a rock wall. Blow it up with a bomb, and you'll enter a room with two Dodongos and a large rock. You'll find this Gold Skulltula on the back of the rock.

After you unlock the Bomb Bag, head back down to the first floor. Follow the path to the east, where you'll find a rock wall. Blow it up, and head inside to find a Gold Skulltula.

After you head through the Dodongo skull, follow the path to the northwestern corner of the area. Move the stone cubes to the rock wall, and blow it up. Defeat the Armos and baby Dodongos inside to find the Gold Skulltula.

Between Dodongo Cavern and Kakariko Village, you'll find a rock wall. Blow it up with a bomb, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula inside.

Head to the top floor of Goron City, and make your way to the west side. You'll find a room full of rocks that you can blow up with your bombs. Blow up two rocks on the right side of the room, and then start making your way to the center of the room. Then, blow up the rocks to the west until you reach the wall. There will be a hopping crate in the northwest corner. Roll into it and a Gold Skulltula will pop out.

Run into Death Mountain Crater, turn left, and quickly roll into the bouncing box to find a Gold Skulltula.

Head to the east side of the first floor of Dodongo Cavern, and you'll find a ledge on your left around halfway down the hallway. Play the Scarecrow Song to summon a scarecrow, and Hookshot to it. You'll find a Gold Skulltula on the wall.

Go down one floor in Goron City, and walk around the ring to the other side. Look at the backside of the central rock, and you'll find the Gold Skulltula. Hit it with your Hookshot to retrieve it.

After you learn the Bolero of Fire outside the Fire Temple, travel back to the past to become young Link. Then, warp back to the Fire Temple, and put a bug onto the soft soil to the south.

After you enter the first eastern room in the Fire Temple, follow the floating platforms in the northeast. The last platform will launch upwards to the higher ledge. Jump over and play the Song of Time to move the cube. Climb onto it and head through the door to enter a room with Flying Tiles, a Like Like, and a Gold Skulltula.

After you reach the third floor, take the path to the north, and you'll find a rock wall. Destroy it, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula.

After you make it through the room with the moving fire wall on the third floor, push the cube out of the wall. Jump to the platform in front of you, and play the Scarecrow Song. Hookshot to it, and then Hookshot to the platform nearby. Go into the room, climb up the wall, and you'll find this Gold Skulltula at the top.

Using the information from the previous Gold Skulltula, use the Scarecrow Song to make it to the fifth floor. You'll find this Gold Skulltula on a wall to the northwest.

After you unlock the Megaton Hammer and destroy the tiered statue near the entrance, make your way to the room in the northeast. You'll find Flying Tiles, a Like Like, and a black rock. Destroy the black rock with the hammer, and you'll find a Gold Skulltula.

This Gold Skulltula is found behind a rock near the top of the Death Mountain Trail. Before you reach the netting to climb up to the crater, you'll find a rock on your right. Break it with the Megaton Hammer as an adult, and a Gold Skulltula will be behind it.

This Gold Skulltula is found behind a black rock on the way to Goron City. The rock is just next to the bomb flower used to blow up the entrance to Dodongo Cavern. Hit the rock with the Megaton Hammer to break it.

The Relationship Between Comedy, Democracy, and AI

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Every month, I spend hundreds of hours and thousands of dollars keeping The Marginalian going. For two decades, it has remained free, ad-free, AI-free, fully human and alive thanks to patronage from readers. I have no staff, no interns, not even an assistant — a thoroughly one-woman labor of love that is also my life and my livelihood. If it makes your own life more livable in any way, please consider aiding its sustenance with a one-time or loyal donation. Your support makes all the difference.

You can also become a spontaneous supporter with a one-time donation in any amount:

Partial to Bitcoin? You can beam some bit-love my way: 197usDS6AsL9wDKxtGM6xaWjmR5ejgqem7

Need to cancel a recurring donation? Go here.

For an act of resistance to the tyranny of algorithms, try the Marginalian newsletter—undistracted notes on the search for meaning, free, ad-free, AI-free, fully human since 2006. (Here is an example.)

“Humor, more than anything else in the human make-up, can afford an aloofness and an ability to rise above any situation, even if only for a few seconds,” Viktor Frankl wrote in recalling the role of humor as a lifeline to sanity and survival in the Holocaust.

Humor is a fundamental human freedom: To laugh is to protect ourselves from all that dehumanizes us — tyranny and transactionalism, captivity and commodification. Humor is also fundamental to free societies, to freedom of thought, to democracy: To laugh together is to harmonize our deepest humanity.

Because humor is the art of surprise, it exists beyond the reach of — and is an antidote to — the artifices of predictive computation we mistake for intelligence.

In her altogether fantastic manifesto for breaking free from the prison of predictive technologies, Carissa Véliz argues that humor thrives in the same “spaces of indeterminacy” where creativity and innovation also thrive. (“Whatever inspiration is, it’s born from a continuous ‘I don’t know,’” the Polish poet Wisława Szymborska observed in her magnificent Nobel Prize acceptance speech.) With an eye to the emotional attunement of sharing laughter, Véliz considers the relationship between humor, democracy, and AI:

Our sense of humor is a dialogue, and when we delegate the conversation to AI, we lose the personal relationship with another human being. Democracy itself is a conversation. If we give up our seat at that table and give it to an AI, we will lose our voice.

Comedy is not inconsequential for democracy. Comedy sets the tone for freedom. It gives us courage. A documentary about comedy clubs in war-torn Ukraine reminds me that humor is not a luxury; it’s indispensable. “When I went to Sarajevo to a film festival,” Cleese says, “people were talking about the early ’90s, when Yugoslavia was falling apart. Sarajevo is down in a valley, and the Serbs were up in the hills throwing shells down, and telescopic snipers were shooting people crossing the street. And they told me that they had found an underground car park, had turned it into a cinema, and they would show comedies, including Monty Python, and they would laugh. When they came out, an hour and a half later, they felt better. Nothing had improved. But they were better able to deal with their situation.” Comedy can be an ally in our challenge to defy the odds.

Humor, like creativity, works by defying expectation. It is a felicitous violation of the norm. Societies thrive and progress by continually challenging the norm, the given, the status quo. Every revolution, every change in regime, is the story of turning the old order from terrible to laughable.

Every month, I spend hundreds of hours and thousands of dollars keeping The Marginalian going. For two decades, it has remained free, ad-free, AI-free, fully human and alive thanks to patronage from readers. I have no staff, no interns, not even an assistant — a thoroughly one-woman labor of love that is also my life and my livelihood. If it makes your own life more livable in any way, please consider aiding its sustenance with a one-time or loyal donation. Your support makes all the difference.

You can also become a spontaneous supporter with a one-time donation in any amount:

Partial to Bitcoin? You can beam some bit-love my way: 197usDS6AsL9wDKxtGM6xaWjmR5ejgqem7

Need to cancel an existing donation? (It's okay — life changes course. I treasure your kindness and appreciate your support for as long as it lasted.) You can do so on this page.

Friday Five With Jiun Ho: A World of Design Inspiration

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

“When I was 8, my mom told me I could be poor in life, but I should never be poor in experiences,” San Francisco-based creative Jiun Ho recalls. “It’s why I travel the way I do, and why I’d rather design something I’ve actually lived than something I saw in a magazine.”

Ho grew up in a lower-middle-class family in rural Kuala Lumpur, Malaysia, in the 1970s, with little exposure to art or architecture. That changed at age 9, when he visited Japan, marking his first trip outside Malaysia.

“I didn’t know interior design was a job,” Ho recalls, “but I knew I’d seen something I couldn’t forget: the buildings, the craft, the care in everything, the sense of history.”

That trip sparked a lifelong love of architecture and design, along with a thirst for travel. Having visited 135 countries and counting, Ho finds inspiration in every trip. “I come home, and I want to make something,” he says.

His first major project brought him full circle: designing the interiors of architect César Pelli’s Petronas Twin Towers in Kuala Lumpur. “Suddenly I was helping design the interiors of a building my whole country was watching go up,” explains Ho, who still counts it among his best projects. “And it was home. A Malaysian kid working on the building that put Kuala Lumpur on the map. You don’t forget that.”

A residential project in Taipei 26 years ago opened another door. After Ho completed the apartment’s interiors, the client asked him to design original furniture, lighting, and rugs. Though he’d never designed furniture before, Ho presented 35 watercolor sketches, and the client wanted every piece produced. “One piece led to the next, then a whole collection,” he says.

Jiun Ho’s sketch of the YuanYang side table. Photography courtesy of Ho.

Since then, Ho’s studio has grown into a global lifestyle brand, designing high-end residential, hospitality, and restaurant interiors while producing furniture, lighting, and textiles for the trade.

For today’s Friday Five, we spent five minutes with Jiun Ho. He shares the inspiration behind three pieces from Unbound, a limited-edition capsule collection released this fall in collaboration with four other designers.

The Whisper Pendant in the Unbound Collection by Jiun Ho. Photography courtesy of Ho.

Ho invited four designers he admires—Francis Nicdao of Pembrooke & Ives, James Hunter of The Wiseman Group, Jamie Drake, and Nicole Fuller—to collaborate on Unbound, with a simple brief: “Nothing bigger than 36 by 36 by 36 inches.” Each designer was asked to create a one-of-a-kind object incorporating their specialty, whether furniture, lighting, or textiles, and one of two crafts: hand-painted wallpaper or cloisonné by Lala Curio, one of Jiun Ho’s creative partners. The collection debuted in September 2026.

On his vision behind the project, Ho says: “The point was to show what we can really do. Our craft. How far we can take customization. And what happens when you put creative people together and say yes to their ideas instead of no.”

Ever the overachiever, Ho designed three objects rather than one, each inspired in some way by his global travels and what he’s learned along the way. Below, Ho shares his inspirations in his own words.

The Yuanyang Hani Rice Terraces in China inspired Jiun Ho. Photography by Ho.

“Yuanyang County is in the Yunnan province of China, where generations of farmers have carved whole mountainsides into rice terraces. Thousands of steps of water and earth, all shaped by hand. To me, it’s one of the most beautiful examples of people and nature working together.”

The YuanYang Side Table by Jiun Ho. Photography courtesy of Ho.

“That’s where the YuanYang side table came from. It’s clad in metal with a cinnamon bronze finish, and the steps down each side are covered in intricate cloisonné. The steps on each echo the terraces, and the cloisonné captures the pattern of the fields catching light.”

The silkworm cocoon also inspired Jiun Ho’s work. Photography by Ho.

“A cocoon is one of nature’s perfect forms. It’s soft and strong, protecting something while it changes. That’s the shape of the Whisper pendant. The surface comes from kintsugi, the Japanese art of repairing broken pottery with gold, where the crack becomes the most beautiful part.”

“We tore handmade Japanese washi paper into pieces, glued them back together, and traced the seams in gold leaf to create a kintsugi-inspired pattern. When it’s lit, it glows like light through silk.”

Whisper Pendant by Jiun Ho. Photography courtesy of Ho.

“In Chinese culture, 8 is the luckiest number. It means good fortune and prosperity. Turn it on its side, and it becomes the symbol for infinity. The Forma pendant comes from that double meaning: good fortune and infinity; luck that never ends. Its shade is painted in gold and silver leaf; the fortune is falling from heaven.

The Forma pendant in the Unbound collection by Jiun Ho. Photography courtesy of Ho.

A 25-plus-year veteran of the interior design industry, Danine Alati began her career at commercial design publication Contract, where she spent 11 years before moving into luxury lifestyle media and working on titles including Hamptons and Art Basel. Based in Brooklyn with her family, she has contributed to AD PRO, DuJour, Galerie, and aspire, among other design and lifestyle publications.

You can follow Danine Alati on Linkedin. Read all of Danine Alati's posts.

The Belgian artist and designer shares what inspired the Anthology Side Table/Stool, created in collaboration with jeweler Lore Van Keer

The San Francisco-based artist finds unexpected connections between science, financial markets, and fashion in her paintings

Drawing on his Mexican heritage, the designer reimagines volcanic stone, copper, and obsidian as contemporary surfaces in a collaboration with WVH

Why a Hyperliquid Treasury Firm Wants to Become a US Futures Broker

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Why a Hyperliquid Treasury Firm Wants to Become a US Futures Broker

Hyperliquid Strategies built its business around holding HYPE. Now it wants to help Americans trade on Hyperliquid, too.

The Nasdaq-listed treasury firm, which trades as PURR and is chaired by former Barclays CEO Bob Diamond, disclosed in its quarterly results Thursday that a subsidiary has applied to become a futures commission merchant, or FCM.

That’s the type of CFTC-registered broker that holds customer funds and handles futures orders. If approved, the unit would carry U.S. client accounts for permissioned markets built on Hyperliquid, letting Americans trade perpetual futures within a regulated framework.

Perpetual futures are derivatives with no expiration date that let traders take leveraged positions. Hyperliquid, a major onchain venue for them, still blocks U.S. users.

Its HIP-3 framework lets outside firms launch their own perpetual markets on Hyperliquid’s order book after staking HYPE. A new permissioned version, called HIP-3*, would let a regulated deployer run markets restricted to approved traders, with Hyperliquid acting as neutral infrastructure.

“We deployed $802 million this quarter and filed an application to become a futures commission merchant,” CEO David Schamis said. “Those two moves are the same bet.”

The firm held about 38.5 million HYPE at the end of September, worth roughly $3.5 billion, and more U.S. trading routed through Hyperliquid would likely mean more fees for the protocol, which uses fee revenue to buy back HYPE. An FCM would also give Hyperliquid Strategies a business of its own beyond holding tokens.

During the quarter, the firm spent $785 million buying 9.1 million HYPE and $17 million repurchasing 2.7 million of its own shares, funded largely by $911 million raised through stock sales. It reported $620.6 million in net income, driven almost entirely by $803 million in unrealized gains as HYPE rose from about $65 to $91.

It won’t be alone. Kraken parent Payward has said it plans to deploy HIP-3* markets through its CFTC-regulated Bitnomial exchange, with NinjaTrader Clearing serving as the FCM.

And last week, NYSE owner Intercontinental Exchange agreed to team up with Trade[XYZ] to bring Hyperliquid-based perpetual futures to the U.S., sending HYPE up 13%. Trade[XYZ] already runs markets on Hyperliquid, including perpetual futures tied to the S&P 500.

Regulators must sign off on both the FCM registration and the permissioned HIP-3 markets it would serve, and the company has given no timeline.

Internet Provider Offers Free Upgrades to Rush GTA 6 Downloads

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Michael Manning (He/Him) has been enamored with video games since he first played the Sonic Classic Collection as a child. With decades of experience in gaming and a full tour as a Gaming Journalist, Michael is ready to step up to the plate. From AAA to the smallest passion projects, his motto is that every piece of ar

With the always-online nature of modern gaming, the internet is a real focus point for players across the globe, and ISPs have become a leading force in gaming, whether they fully step into the space or not.

With two new squadmates, a Long-War style campaign, and a collection of community-led fixes, MENACE is always looking up.

With the lack of actual physical discs able to house the entirety of a modern AAA game, and the spread of download code purchases, solid internet has become all but a requirement to keep up with gaming, even if you’re playing something completely single-player.

Between day-one patches and massive file sizes, slow internet can totally curb the excitement of snagging a new game, adding hours or even days before you’re free to actually play. By jumping in to assist with this problem, Spectrum is definitely earning some points with the general public, an easy win to course-correct some of the annoyances users complain about with the service.

Whether it's for connecting to online lobbies or downloading massive games, ISPs have become a leading force in gaming, whether they fully step into the space or not.

GTA 6 was recently revealed to stack up at a whopping 212 GBs, a solid size for a game of its hype and magnitude, sure to only bloat as the eventual inclusion of multiplayer and extra content looms. For now, though, players are incredibly eager for more about Grand Theft Auto 6’s story, and following spoiler lockdowns from Rockstar, the only place to learn will be by playing.

With that in mind, the scramble to download the game will put some major pressure on both the Microsoft and PlayStation stores, just hopefully not enough to lead to any kind of store outage. Steam has fallen victim to such overuse during major drops like Hollow Knight: Silksong, and even if players have super-fast Wi-Fi, if the store is down, the store is down.

This is the precarious position of a fully online gaming space: only so much bandwidth to go around, and it just adds another thing to pay for to keep up with gaming. Massive downloads and always-on functionality are the reality of the space now, and despite the misgivings, developers have used them to make major strides.

A larger focus on always-online functionality means developers have gotten extremely creative with it over the years, leading to some of the best mechanics in the games they’re in. Death Stranding is one title that makes fantastic use both narratively and mechanically of being always connected with the world at large, but it's hardly the first to do so.

The Souls series’ message system, allowing you to get advice and tricks from anyone else, and even see how they died, is a major mechanic throughout their titles, one that would make the series feel a little hollow if it up and vanished. Despite the pains of maintaining a connection, these titles are genuinely stronger for having it, despite any misgivings or annoyances.

GTA 6 is destined to have a massive online space once its standalone online mode drops, if the sheer popularity of GTA 5 Online is any indication. For now, though, any boost to connection will simply make getting the game easier, a boon many are sure to snatch up as soon as the offer is fully on the table. Everyone wants in on the PR pie of GTA 6, even if their inclusion feels a little out of left field.

STJV, a French union representing members of Arkane Lyon, accuses Microsoft of major interference with the project.

Google's eyewear partners can't keep a lid on its smart glasses plans

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Gentle Monster shared that its “Intelligent Eyewear” will be available to pre-order on November 12.

Gentle Monster has announced its version of Google's Android XR smart glasses will launch in December, and its website says pre-orders open on November 12, the same date Warby Parker appeared to accidentally share on Thursday. Both eyewear brands, along with Samsung, are Google's partners on the project, which is set up to be both a competitor to Meta's smart glasses and a revival (of a sort) of Google Glass. Google itself has yet to confirm either date.

The Korean brand paired its announcement with a teaser of a short film directed by Jonathan Glazer, best known for Under the Skin and The Zone of Interest, and starring Joaquin Phoenix, who famously played a man who falls in love with an AI in Her. Gentle Monster says a full version of the short will be available in mid-November. The teaser suggests it'll skew much more toward experimental film than a straightforward commercial.

Gentle Monster's first Intelligent Eyewear collection ⠀ Joaquin Phoenix stars in a short film directed by Jonathan Glazer—a cinematic journey between reality and dream. Full film releasing mid-November. ⠀ The collection launches this December. ⠀ Register for the collection... pic.twitter.com/UBv6pdHWSW

— GENTLE MONSTER (@_GentleMonster_) November 6, 2026

Google's Android XR platform is designed to power everything from the mixed reality experience on the Samsung Galaxy XR to the augmented reality one offered on the Xreal Aura. Gentle Monster and Warby Parker's glasses will provide a display-free, audio-first version of the operating system, but should offer similar access to Gemini.

It's not clear how this "Intelligent Eyewear," as Google and its partners call it, will go over, but Google seems to be hoping Meta's strategy of packaging tech in a desirable brand will help counter any backlash. With both Warby Parker and Gentle Monster's launches on the calendar, we won't have long to wait to see if it works.

Stellar Activates Protocol 30 Bell Testnet, Begins Testing Quantum-Resistant Signatures

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Stellar’s Bell upgrade reached its next major milestone today, according to the data shared by the Stellar Development Foundation. The Bell (Protocol 30 upgrade) went live on the testnet on Tuesday, November 3. The update rolled out smart-account features for enterprises and post-quantum signature verification, enhancing transaction and security efficiency.

As announced today, Stellar activated the Bell upgrade on its testnet (test network) on Nov. 3. Stellar’s move to enable Bell integration on the testnet is a crucial step as it prepares for a Mainnet vote set for Nov. 23.

Bell’s activation on the testnet allows developers to test the upgrade’s capabilities. Through the test environment, developers explore the capabilities of new smart contract features for businesses and the effectiveness of post-quantum signature verification.

The Bell upgrade, also commonly recognized as Protocol 30, aims to improve smart account capabilities for businesses. At the same time, it prepares Stellar’s infrastructure for future security requirements, including quantum-safe signatures and faster ledgers.

Its mainnet launch is scheduled for Nov. 23, subject to community voting, which could shape the future capabilities and direction of Stellar’s blockchain technology. With the upgrade, businesses can use more sophisticated, efficient accounts, while quantum security remains a long-term necessity.

Stellar entered an important phase on Tuesday as the Bell upgrade went live on its testnet. The upgrade represents another step in the blockchain’s commitment to supporting financial utilities and institutional use cases.

The Protocol 30 upgrade seeks to expand what enterprises can do with accounts on the Stellar blockchain and also lays the foundation for quantum-safe digital signatures.

Digital signatures are important for blockchain security as they allow customers to authorize transactions and prove ownership and control of their accounts.

Advancing quantum computing poses a serious threat to cryptographic blockchains. This explains why Stellar is preparing to make its network quantum-resistant to address such potential risks.

Wrap yourself in a cozy atmosphere where every small action and detail matters. Hozy is about slowing down. There are no timers, scores, penalties, and absolutely no stress or pressure. Just you, your tools, and the simple joy of making spaces beautiful again. Whether you’re wiping windows, repainting walls, or finding the perfect spot for a sofa, everything is designed to keep you in a calm, satisfying flow.

Skip the endless catalogue overwhelm. Hozy offers a carefully curated selection of furniture and decor for each level that work well together. Place items freely, rotate and arrange them however you like—there is no right or wrong and no room is ever the same. Use the tools at your disposal wisely, and you’ll create spaces worthy of an interior-design magazine!

Macklemore Says Nine U.S. Venues Turned Down ‘Free Palestine’ Tour: ‘They Got Me F-cked Up’

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Last month, Macklemore launched his Free Palestine tour in response to his removal from Ed Sheeran’s tour at the behest of stadium owners who opposed his vocal support of Palestinians in Gaza. It proved to be a success, with three sold-out shows in Paris, Dublin, and London. Confident fans would show up in support and solidarity, he made plans to bring the tour stateside — but he claims nine different U.S. venues turned him down.

“In over twenty years of touring, I’ve never experienced anything like this,” Macklemore wrote on Instagram. The outreach process began weeks ago, he claims, and “by mid-September, we received available dates at these arenas through the end of the year. But once they found out these were my benefit shows for Palestine, those dates were suddenly off the table. The doors were closed. The conversations were all different, but the result was the same. The shows were not going to be allowed to happen in these venues.”

The named locations include Madison Square Garden, Barclays Center, and UBS Arena in New York; Prudential Center in New Jersey; Intuit Dome, Kia Forum, and Crypto.com Arena in Los Angeles; Chase Center in San Francisco; and Climate Pledge Arena in Seattle, the rapper’s hometown.

Following publication of this article, an MSG Entertainment spokesperson tells Rolling Stone, “We have some of the busiest venues in the world with extremely limited availability and he’s not an arena-level act.” (Representatives for the other venues did not immediately return Rolling Stone‘s request for comment.)

“The trouble with buying power is believing you’ve bought the people too. The billionaires depend on our silence and complicity. They forget that we buy their tickets. We fill their arenas. We build their empires. And we have the power to bring them down,” the rapper added. “The people have spoken. We want an end to the genocide and we want a Free Palestine. The arenas have made their decision. Now we’ll make ours. If they think the threat of never playing another arena in America will make me retreat into silence, they got me fucked up.” Editor’s picks The 250 Greatest Albums of the 21st Century So Far The 100 Best TV Episodes of All Time The 500 Greatest Albums of All Time 100 Best Movies of the 21st Century

In September, the promoter of the U.S. leg of Sheeran’s Loop Tour said venues booked for the tour notified them “that they will not allow a concert to take place with Macklemore on the lineup.” Robert Kraft, who owns Gillette Stadium just outside of Boston, confirmed as much, saying the location is committed to “providing a welcoming environment for all guests and ensuring that all events held at our venue do not provide a platform for hate speech.”

“I’d rather lose every opportunity in this industry than spend another day protecting people who value their business relationships with Israel more than they value Palestinian human life,” Macklemore said. “We live in a country that prides itself on freedom of speech. Yet we’ve reached a point where a handful of billionaires can decide which causes are acceptable, which voices are welcome and which human lives are worth defending. Today it’s Palestine. What will it be tomorrow? To these owners, the words Free Palestine represent a threat. Not because they endanger anyone, but because those words challenge the systems of power they have built their fortunes on.”

Sheeran argued that the decision to remove Macklemore as an opener, backed by the venues and promoters, came down to not wanting to disappoint fans or impact the other people working on the tour by cancelling. The explanation weakened as the rest of his opening acts withdrew in solidarity, as well as his backing band, Beoga, who joined Macklemore during the Dublin concert on the Free Palestine tour. A wave of artists spoke out in defense of free speech and artistic expression; and when it came time to back Macklemore’s tour, acts like Hozier and Kneecap joined eagerly. Related Content Trump’s First Big Post-Midterms Move Is Promoting Stephen Miller Trump Launches Triumphal Arch Website Right As Judge Blocks Construction Thomas Markle Sr. Arrested Amid Allegations of Child Sex Abuse Images Dolly Parton's Manager Dismisses Lawsuit Against Her Nephew

“To the artists who are afraid of what those two words [“Free Palestine”] might cost them, I understand that fear. I felt it myself, especially when I was still wrestling with whether to speak out. But I believe that fear begins to lose its power the moment we realize we’re not standing alone,” Macklemore said. “I dream of a world where saying Free Palestine is not controversial. It is expected. Where artists value their freedom of speech and collective liberation more than their brand deals.”

He added, “Look at what just happened in Europe. Three sold-out arenas. Tens of thousands of people from different backgrounds, religions and walks of life standing together. More than forty artists and speakers putting their names and platforms behind Palestinian liberation. For all the attempts to paint this movement as one rooted in hatred, what filled those rooms was love.”

Proceeds from the Free Palestine Tour benefitted a collection of organizations supporting Palestinians in Gaza, including Medical Aid for Palestinians, Palestinian Youth Movement, Heal Palestine, Palestine Children’s Relief Fund, Gaza Soup Kitchen, Jewish Voices for Peace, and the UNRWA USA National Committee.

In his statement, Macklemore emphasized that the support of this movement extends far beyond music. He mentioned Michigan senator-elect Abdul El-Sayed, who “won his Senate race on a platform that included ending the genocide in Gaza and stopping U.S. arms sales to Israel.” (Humanitarian groups and a United Nations commission have said Israel’s actions in Gaza constitute a genocide. Israel and its allies, including the United States, have denied this.)

A ceasefire agreement approved by Israel took effect on Oct. 10, 2025. The Associated Press reports over 1,400 have been killed in Gaza since then, with the total Palestinian death toll since October 2023 rising to more than 74,000. Millions of others have been displaced due to the destruction of the landscape. Trending Stories A Jesuit Priest Explains How He Facilitated Bob Dylan’s Meeting With the Pope Appeals Court Says Lawsuit Over ‘The Pitt’ Can Proceed, Cites ‘Numerous’ Similarities to ‘ER’ Adam Lambert on His Future With Queen, New Music, and Why the Show Must Go On Pink Floyd Mark ‘Animals’ 50th Anniversary With Reissue Featuring Unreleased Music

Macklemore is committed to continuing to raise funds and stand in solidarity with Palestinians in Gaza through both speech and performance, even if he doesn’t yet know where those will be allowed to happen. “We’ll keep working with other artists and organizers, continuing where we left off in Europe. We’ll probably just take that shit outside where it belonged in the first place,” he said. “There comes a point when the world changes, not because those in power give us permission, but because the people stop asking for it. We will build our own stages. They may own the arenas. But they do not own the people who fill them. And they never will.”

U.S. Now Requires Ebola Attestation For Flights From Abroad — How To Complete It And Who Can Be Denied Boarding

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

Flying to the U.S. from abroad now requires an Ebola attestation. You have to confirm that you haven’t been in the Democratic Republic of the Congo during the previous 21 days before boarding your flight even if you’ve never been anywhere near Africa. An internal American Airlines memo explains how they’re collecting it, including what happens when a kiosk sends you to your phone and what to do if you can’t use the app. For most passengers, this is another check-in step. For someone who’s recently been to Congo, it can mean being unable to board a flight home even with a U.S. passport. Who Has To Complete An Ebola Attestation? The federal requirement took effect for flights departing for the United States at or after 11:59 p.m. Eastern Standard Time on November 3, 2026. It applies across airlines, including foreign airlines. You provide a declaration about recent travel history. That’s it – there’s no Ebola test or vaccination certificate. U.S. citizens, foreign visitors and green card holders are covered. Passengers merely connecting through the United States are covered, too. Departures from airports with U.S. immigration preclearance still count. Domestic flights aren’t covered. U.S. territories aren’t foreign countries under this rule, so a nonstop flight from Puerto Rico to the mainland doesn’t trigger it. The form requires an attestation for each individual, including children. A parent or another authorized representative can complete it for a child or someone unable to do it themselves. The current order runs through 11:59 p.m. Eastern Standard Time on December 18, 2026. The March 31, 2027 date printed on the form is the expiration of its paperwork approval, not the scheduled end of the travel requirement. How To Complete It On American Airlines American’s October 30 internal advisory lays out the airport procedures. Use the app or aa.com during check-in. The app requires you to complete the attestation before you can finish checking in. Once submitted, American adds a completion marker to your reservation. Don’t expect the kiosk to collect it. American says kiosks don’t support attestation collection. They’ll display a QR code directing you to the mobile app, and you’ll need to finish the attestation before completing kiosk check-in. No working phone? Ask for paper. American allows passengers to bring a completed paper form or request one at the airport. You can download the official fillable form here. An agent can also record a verbal attestation. You must first review the attestation wording. The agent then asks you to confirm your travel history and records that you reviewed the language and attested. Both American’s memo and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention’s instructions permit this. For paper, enter the name, select the appropriate option for yourself or someone you’re authorized to represent, and sign and date it. Read both pages and give the completed form to the airline. American must retain completed paper attestations for 42 days. If the app won’t let you proceed, ask an agent to check whether the attestation is recorded. American calls the completion marker ADOC. Ideally complete this before heading to the airport. If the technology fails, ask for the paper form. If You’ve Been To Congo, The Form Can Stop Your Trip The question is whether you’ve been physically present in the Democratic Republic of the Congo, not whether you feel healthy or believe you were exposed. Even a connection at a Congolese airport counts, including a stop where you stayed on the aircraft. The State Department confirms that U.S. citizens are also barred from boarding commercial flights to the United States during the waiting period. Limited government exemptions exist, including authorized medical transport and limited humanitarian cases. The calendar calculation is easy to mess up: Day 0: The day you leave Congo. Days 1–21: The full calendar days you remain outside the country. Day 22: The first day you can board a flight to the United States, assuming you meet other entry requirements. Leave Congo on November 1, for instance, and the first eligible boarding date is November 23. The CDC illustrates the waiting period this way: Uganda and South Sudan have separate rules. Under the current entry restrictions, non-U.S. citizens and non-U.S. nationals, including green card holders, who’ve recently been in either country must also wait outside the United States. U.S. citizens and nationals who’ve been there, but not Congo, can return through New York JFK, Washington Dulles or Atlanta for public health screening. Airport connections count for these rules as well. How Serious Is The Ebola Outbreak? The CDC’s November 3 update, reporting data through November 1 lists 10,563 confirmed cases and 5,130 confirmed deaths almost all in Congo which is the second-largest Ebola outbreak recorded. Outbreak Cases Deaths 2014–2016 West Africa epidemic About 28,600 reported About 11,300 2018–2020 Congo outbreak 3,470 reported 2,287 2026 outbreak, through November 1 10,563 confirmed 5,130 confirmed he current outbreak also passed 1,000 confirmed cases within 40 days of response activation; the 2018 outbreak took about 235 days. Conflict, weak health services and difficulty tracing contacts have helped it spread. Kenya and Nigeria reported imported cases in October. Ebola is extremely serious if you become infected, but the risk of becoming infected is low. The CDC reports no U.S. cases associated with this outbreak and considers introduction into the United States very unlikely. (But they aren’t always right about these things…) Ebola spreads through infected blood and other body fluids. It’s not airborne and it isn’t believed to spread by pre-symptomatic people. Taking a flight with someone exposed to Ebola isn’t going to get you sick. You need exposure to their fluids. Then illness can progress from fever, aches and fatigue to severe vomiting, diarrhea, dehydration and organ failure. The outbreak’s reported fatality rate has been around half (but probably overstates things because those dying of Ebola wind up as identified cases, while many who don’t die likely to do not). When 2021 Ebola travel restrictions were put in place, there were approved vaccines and antibody treatments. Those were for the Zaire type of Ebola while this is caused by Bundibugyo virus, for which there’s no approved vaccine or specific treatment. How Much Will The Attestation Help? Ebola can take up to 21 days to cause symptoms. Someone can be infected, feel well and pass a temperature check while still incubating the disease. Waiting after leaving the outbreak country gives that infection time to become apparent before a U.S. flight. During the earlier epidemic, about 38,000 travelers received enhanced assessments at U.S. ports of entry. One was later diagnosed with Ebola who had no symptoms when he arrived and reported his illness afterward. A declaration’s effectiveness depends on truthful reporting. Requiring three weeks of extra time outside the United States creates an incentive for untruthful reporting. What To Do If You’re Restricted From Flying If you’re barred because of recent travel to an affected country, the Transportation Department’s October 28 enforcement notice preserves passenger protections: For a communicable-disease travel refusal, the passenger can choose qualifying rebooking within 90 days without penalty at the original applicable fare, or a refund for unused flights. A required significant itinerary change, such as rerouting for health screening, triggers a refund if you decline the changed trip. The separate public health travel restriction rules provide transferable credits valid for at least five years. You select the applicable refund or credit remedy, rather than collect both. If you’re refused travel on this basis, get it in writing. The airline must provide that explanation within 10 days of your request. Leave Congo on November 1, for instance, and the first eligible boarding date is November 23. The CDC illustrates the waiting period this way: Uganda and South Sudan have separate rules. Under the current entry restrictions, non-U.S. citizens and non-U.S. nationals, including green card holders, who’ve recently been in either country must also wait outside the United States. U.S. citizens and nationals who’ve been there, but not Congo, can return through New York JFK, Washington Dulles or Atlanta for public health screening. Airport connections count for these rules as well. How Serious Is The Ebola Outbreak? Ebola is extremely serious if you become infected, but the risk of becoming infected is low. The CDC reports no U.S. cases associated with this outbreak and considers introduction into the United States very unlikely. (But they aren’t always right about these things…) Ebola spreads through infected blood and other body fluids. It’s not airborne and it isn’t believed to spread by pre-symptomatic people. Taking a flight with someone exposed to Ebola isn’t going to get you sick. You need exposure to their fluids. Then illness can progress from fever, aches and fatigue to severe vomiting, diarrhea, dehydration and organ failure. The outbreak’s reported fatality rate has been around half (but probably overstates things because those dying of Ebola wind up as identified cases, while many who don’t die likely to do not). Those were for the Zaire type of Ebola while this is caused by Bundibugyo virus, for which there’s no approved vaccine or specific treatment. How Much Will The Attestation Help? What To Do If You’re Restricted From Flying If you’re barred because of recent travel to an affected country, the Transportation Department’s October 28 enforcement notice preserves passenger protections: For a communicable-disease travel refusal, the passenger can choose qualifying rebooking within 90 days without penalty at the original applicable fare, or a refund for unused flights. A required significant itinerary change, such as rerouting for health screening, triggers a refund if you decline the changed trip. The separate public health travel restriction rules provide transferable credits valid for at least five years. You select the applicable refund or credit remedy, rather than collect both. If you’re refused travel on this basis, get it in writing. The airline must provide that explanation within 10 days of your request. The federal requirement took effect for flights departing for the United States at or after 11:59 p.m. Eastern Standard Time on November 3, 2026. It applies across airlines, including foreign airlines.

You provide a declaration about recent travel history. That’s it – there’s no Ebola test or vaccination certificate.

The form requires an attestation for each individual, including children. A parent or another authorized representative can complete it for a child or someone unable to do it themselves.

The current order runs through 11:59 p.m. Eastern Standard Time on December 18, 2026. The March 31, 2027 date printed on the form is the expiration of its paperwork approval, not the scheduled end of the travel requirement.

American’s October 30 internal advisory lays out the airport procedures. Use the app or aa.com during check-in. The app requires you to complete the attestation before you can finish checking in. Once submitted, American adds a completion marker to your reservation. Don’t expect the kiosk to collect it. American says kiosks don’t support attestation collection. They’ll display a QR code directing you to the mobile app, and you’ll need to finish the attestation before completing kiosk check-in. No working phone? Ask for paper. American allows passengers to bring a completed paper form or request one at the airport. You can download the official fillable form here. An agent can also record a verbal attestation. You must first review the attestation wording. The agent then asks you to confirm your travel history and records that you reviewed the language and attested. Both American’s memo and the Centers for Disease Control and Prevention’s instructions permit this. For paper, enter the name, select the appropriate option for yourself or someone you’re authorized to represent, and sign and date it. Read both pages and give the completed form to the airline. American must retain completed paper attestations for 42 days. If the app won’t let you proceed, ask an agent to check whether the attestation is recorded. American calls the completion marker ADOC. Ideally complete this before heading to the airport. If the technology fails, ask for the paper form. Will Lankshear earns eye-catching Man Utd claim after Middlesbrough rise

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

EFL expert Adam Virgo has claimed that Middlesbrough star Will Lankshear would get into the current Manchester United starting lineup.

The former Tottenham Hotspur striker is enjoying a strong start to the campaign at the Riverside after making the summer switch to Kim Hellberg’s side.

The 21-year-old has so far scored 13 goals from 13 appearances in the Championship, with the Teesside outfit in the mix for a top two place in these early months of the season.

This has led to calls for him to potentially be called up to the England national team, with many comparing his goal scoring feats to previous second division stars like Aleksander Mitrovic and Ivan Toney.

The Serbian broke the EFL goal scoring record when he helped Fulham earn promotion in 2022, finding the back of the net a remarkable 43 times.

There is still a long way to go in the current campaign, but it’s a sign of Lankshear’s performances so far that he is being mentioned in the same breath as the current record holder.

Aaron McClean and Adam Virgo both debated what Premier League clubs Lankshear would be capable of starting in this season, with the former responding positively when asked about potentially getting into the Man United team.

The former suggested that Spurs could use him in their team as well, having only agreed to sell the striker to Boro last summer.

“He’s left Tottenham, and I watched Tottenham on the weekend against [Crystal Palace], I think it just proves again why Tottenham have just struggled in front of goal, in terms of the amount of chances that they’ve created,” said Virgo, via the Inside the 72 podcast.

Stoke City and Middlesbrough have hit gold after utilising the American market to their advantage

“Would he get in the Spurs side now, yes or no?”

“And then we’re looking at teams that maybe looking at strikers, could he get into these clubs: Arsenal?" asked Virgo.

“So you don’t think Everton need a striker?” continued Virgo.

“Well they’ve got one striker at the moment, I don’t think he walks straight in,” replied McClean.

“Could he walk into the Man United team at the moment?

“That’s interesting. I mean, Sunderland, yes. Coventry, yes. Hull, yes. Forest, Palace, Brighton,” said Virgo.

“I don’t think he gets in Brighton, bearing in mind they’re the top scorers in the league at the moment. I don’t think he walks straight in,” answered McClean.

Would Will Lankshear get into the Man United team? Comment your opinion below

Lankshear has a non-penalty expected goals (xG) of 0.88 per 90 from 13 appearances for Boro so far this season (all stats from Fotmob).

While he has over-performed expectations, he is still putting up quite impressive figures, taking a total of 4.12 shots each game, 2.15 of which are on target.

Middlesbrough agreed a deal with Spurs worth a total of £20 million for Lankshear, with the club hoping that he could be a difference-maker in their push for promotion.

And the forward has made a big impact so far, as Hellberg’s side go into the final weekend before the November international break sitting top of the Championship table.

The Teesside outfit have earned 28 points from their first 14 fixtures, putting them one point clear of second place Wolves.

Next up for Boro is a trip to face Watford on Saturday in a 3pm kick-off at Vicarage Road.

Lankshear’s previous loan spell at Oxford United last season showed a lot of promise, as he scored 11 goals from 44 appearances, but he has already surpassed that tally for Boro.

The forward has taken his game to another level in the Championship, and potentially is ready to compete in the Premier League.

The 21-year-old has shown for Hellberg’s side what Spurs are missing, with Roberto de Zerbi’s side particularly struggling to find a convincing number nine to lead the line.

Whether or not he could walk into the current Man United team perhaps depends on the fitness of Benjamin Sesko, who is currently out until January, as they otherwise lack a real striker in their squad.

We want to hear from you. Share your perspective in the comments below, and please keep the conversation respectful.

Designers Reveal the Fastest Way They’ve Updated a Living Room Under $100

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

A few small swaps can make your space feel brand new.

A living room is arguably the most popular spot of the house, so why not give it the tender loving care it deserves with a design refresh? Problem is, when it comes to most home projects, it’s easy to convince yourself good things only come to those who wait—or anyone willing to fork over a small fortune.

Fortunately, affordability and efficiency are no longer mutually exclusive. According to the pros, it’s possible to elevate a space swiftly and without spending much money. Want proof? Keep scrolling for the clever (and quick) ways designers have upgraded a living room for $100 or less.

Barbra Fordyce of Ginger Lemon Indigo says plants are “hands-down” one of the best low-cost, high-impact ways to spruce up your space. “Bringing something living into your living room instantly changes the mood, quite literally bringing life, nature and an organic beauty into the room,” explains the designer. While fresh foliage can look good anywhere, she loves how plants can add a “verdant richness” to neutral rooms without throwing off the entire color palette.

That said, houseplants aren’t a one-size-fits-all situation, so choose wisely.

“If you have high ceilings, you want the plant to be proportional to those dimensions,” Fordyce says. “If the plant or tree is too small in a large living room, it will feel dwarfed.”

For the perfect finishing touch, use your leftover budget to buy a beautiful planter. “The container should echo the feeling of your room,” she shares. “A basket, ceramic container or even a vintage wooden crate can all make beautiful ‘pots’ for your plants.”

Though there is such a thing as too many throw pillows, the right ones can make a difference. “A pillow and throw refresh can go a long way in bringing new life to your sofa,” explains Natalie Kaelin, an interior designer in Raleigh, North Carolina.

Of course, there’s no need to buy brand-new lumbar and square cushions on a whim: Simply purchase covers for your existing pillows and swap them out seasonally.

Neutral linen layers can conjure coastal grandmother vibes virtually anywhere, while plush, jewel-toned velvets offer an elegant, autumnal look. Come winter, embrace the holiday spirit with flannel fabric or a plaid repeat.

We might spend a lot of time talking about layered lighting or how to pick the perfect fixture, but we cannot stress it enough: Pay attention to your bulbs.

“Daylight LED bulbs are incredibly white, so they put out light akin to doctors’ offices and hospitals,” says Elizabeth Cross-Beard of Palm Henri in Baltimore. “A warmer temperate bulb gives a home a candle-light, cozy feel.” As a general rule of thumb, she recommends buying lightbulbs with a temperature of 2700K and avoiding anything above 4000K.

Cross-Beard says switching your bulbs can effectively “redesign” your space, and it’s shockingly inexpensive. “Depending on how many lights you have, it can cost as little as $50,” she adds.

If you want to upgrade your space without spending a cent, Blair Burton says to start with your furniture.

“Sometimes simply pulling furniture away from the walls and creating a more comfortable conversation area can completely change how a room feels,” the Austin-based designer explains.

After all, just because an armchair has been in the corner of your living room for years doesn’t mean it needs to go there. By doing a room rearrangement, you might end up using it more when it’s in front of a warm, sun-drenched window. Maybe you’ll even realize you need to pick up an ottoman to create the ultimate cozy space.

Another $0 hack? Burton also encourages her clients to “shop their homes.” “It’s practical, of course, but it's also about honoring the things they've collected and loved over the years,” she explains. “Sometimes finding an unexpected new home for a favorite piece is one of the most satisfying discoveries in a project.”

“Adding artwork, whether it's a gallery wall, a single large-scale piece, or something in between, makes a huge visual and personal impact in a living room,” says Molly Torres Portnof of DATE Interiors in New York.

Retailers like Minted and Society6 offer well-priced prints, but you can also reframe photographs and posters you already own. As long as you pay attention to scale and proportion, the ceiling’s really the limit. “Rules are meant to be broken,” Portnof says. “If it feels right and makes you happy, you're on the right track.”

If your living room is blessed with beautiful built-ins, you can fill them with treasures you already own, or buy a few new pieces for well under $100.

“Take the time to create vignettes,” advises Tennessee designer Katie Kiser. “More often than not, I find my clients have all the pieces for a well-choreographed space, but they're styled improperly.”

For an instant yet affordable upgrade, head over to your local antique store to pick up some art books, jars, or a trinket tray.

Your Customers Are Using AI Search. They’re Still Googling You, Too. Here’s What Your Marketing Strategy Might Be Missing.

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

You're viewing the US website. Looking for the Site edition?

A new audience-overlap finding is a useful corrective to the idea that AI search is replacing Google. And it changes how businesses should think about discovery and measurement.

Opinions expressed by Entrepreneur contributors are their own.

I keep hearing a version of the same question from business leaders: Is AI search taking customers away from Google?

A new Similarweb audience-overlap figure offers a useful answer, with an important limit. In September, 93.7% of ChatGPT.com’s visitors also visited Google.com. The overlap ran the other way, too: 14.1% of Google.com’s visitors also visited ChatGPT.com, according to Search Engine Watch’s report.

That is not proof that someone asked ChatGPT about a product, then searched for it on Google. The data counts people who visited both websites during the month. It doesn’t show which one they used first, how often they searched, what they searched for or whether they bought anything. It also doesn’t capture the full use of either service through apps.

Those limits matter. But they don’t make the finding irrelevant. They tell us not to mistake channel overlap for channel substitution.

For years, marketers have organized discovery into tidy channels: search, social, referrals, paid media. AI assistants make that division harder to defend. A person might ask an AI tool to compare options, search a brand name in Google, watch a creator review, then visit a company’s site directly. The journey may cross several systems before the business gets a measurable click.

The Similarweb figure doesn’t prove that sequence. It does show why we shouldn’t assume that a visitor counted in an AI audience is a new person who has never used Google. Treating those groups as separate pools can lead to double-counting, inflated reach estimates and bad budget decisions.

I saw a version of this mistake when search was still new to many companies. Some treated SEO as a separate experiment instead of infrastructure for demand that already existed. The risk now is the reverse: treating AI discovery as a replacement for the search habits customers still have.

The better question isn’t which platform wins. It’s whether your brand is present, clear and credible when a customer moves between them.

The overlap stat also exposes a measurement problem. A customer might encounter a brand in an AI answer and later search for it by name. That later visit could show up as Google organic traffic, even if the earlier discovery helped create the demand. Or the AI interaction might never produce a visit at all. The audience-overlap data can’t tell us which happened.

That is why last-click reporting is a poor referee for this shift. A referral source can tell you where a session came from. It usually can’t tell you which earlier interactions made the brand worth looking up.

Google has begun giving site owners a clearer view of one part of the picture. Its Search Console generative AI performance report shows impressions for pages appearing in Google’s AI search features, including AI Overviews and AI Mode. That is useful visibility data, not proof of incremental traffic or revenue, and it doesn’t cover ChatGPT or other AI platforms.

Businesses should keep the measures separate. Track traditional search clicks, branded searches, qualified leads and sales. Alongside them, monitor which pages appear in Google’s AI features and whether your brand is cited in a consistent set of relevant AI prompts. Add a simple question to lead forms or post-purchase surveys: “Where did you first hear about us?” None of those signals proves, on its own, that AI caused a sale. Together, they can reveal patterns that last-click reports miss.

The response isn’t to split the marketing budget evenly between Google and AI. It’s to stop planning as if every platform reaches a different customer.

Keep investing in the parts of search that help customers find and evaluate you. Make sure the pages that explain your products, pricing and value are current. Understand which questions bring qualified prospects to your site. Then extend the same discipline to AI discovery: Check whether answer engines describe your business accurately, cite useful sources and include you when customers compare options.

Most importantly, connect the teams and dashboards. If SEO reports a decline in clicks while AI visibility rises, don’t declare one channel the winner and the other the loser. Look at branded search, lead quality, direct visits and conversion trends. Ask customers what influenced them. Compare the pattern over time, not just across one month’s traffic report.

I don’t read the 93.7% figure as evidence that Google is safe or that AI search is harmless to existing traffic. It doesn’t answer either question. I read it as a reason to be more precise: Customers can add a new way to discover a business without abandoning the old one.

Your customers may meet you in an AI answer and Google you before they act. The companies that understand both moments will make better decisions than the ones still asking which channel gets credit for the whole journey.

I keep hearing a version of the same question from business leaders: Is AI search taking customers away from Google?

A new Similarweb audience-overlap figure offers a useful answer, with an important limit. In September, 93.7% of ChatGPT.com’s visitors also visited Google.com. The overlap ran the other way, too: 14.1% of Google.com’s visitors also visited ChatGPT.com, according to Search Engine Watch’s report.

That is not proof that someone asked ChatGPT about a product, then searched for it on Google. The data counts people who visited both websites during the month. It doesn’t show which one they used first, how often they searched, what they searched for or whether they bought anything. It also doesn’t capture the full use of either service through apps.

OpenAI bugging suspect Alfozan pleads not guilty

📅 2026-11-06  |  Read Source →

The Saudi national charged with planting a listening device in the headquarters of OpenAI while he worked there pleaded not guilty on Friday during an appearance in San Francisco federal court.

Abdulrahman Alfozan, 35, was sent back to jail after the brief hearing. Alfozan has been held without bail since his arrest on a criminal complaint in early October.

He faces a maximum possible sentence of five years in prison if convicted in the case.

A grand jury indicted Alfozan on Oct. 27 on a single count of interception of oral communications, court records show.

Prosecutors say that he put a recording device in a television in an executive conference room at OpenAI's offices in San Francisco on Sept. 28.

Alfozan's lawyer declined to comment after the hearing before Magistrate Judge Peter Kang.

The judge ordered Alfozan to next appear in court on Nov. 17.

Alfozan is a venture partner in a Saudi Arabian venture capital firm, Raed Ventures.

"He currently serves as Engineering Lead at OpenAI, with a focus on AI Agents and the data platform," Alfozan's bio page on Raed's site says.

Prince Khalid Bin Bader Al Saud, a member of Saudi Arabia's royal family, is a venture partner in the same firm.

Alfozan's case comes as U.S. companies such as OpenAI are making rapid advancements in developing artificial intelligence agents, and as concerns grow about the potential theft of AI tech from those companies.In June, President Donald Trump, in an executive order on AI innovation, said it was U.S. policy "to protect American ingenuity and intellectual property from exploitation and theft by adversaries; and to cultivate America's advanced AI-enabled capabilities."Trump has had a close relationship with Saudi Arabia. But he has also taken steps during his second term in the White House to limit the number of visas for highly skilled foreign workers.OpenAI earlier this year held discussions with Saudi Arabia's Public Investment Fund, the country's sovereign wealth fund, about investing in the company.

— Jordan Novet reported from San Francisco
